# Agents · S · part 49

916 entries, strategy-research to studio-junior-dev. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **strategy-research**: Strategy Research & Backtesting — Backtesting, hyperparameter optimization, regime detection ([vortsghost2025/kucoin-lane](https://github.com/vortsghost2025/kucoin-lane/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/strategy-research.md) · ★2)
- **strategy-researcher**: Strategic-theory keeper and consultant for the Mana Map pilot subsystem — "the brains of the operation". Two modes (the spawning prompt MUST state which): MODE research (online research that expands data/strategy/strategy.md + CHANGELOG.md) and MODE consult (RAG-grounded strategic feedback on cards, combos, board states, and whole decks, citing strategy:&lt;id&gt; sections). Consulted by the other pilot agents; produces the strategic frame for a deck. ([macrae/mana-map](https://github.com/macrae/mana-map/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/strategy-researcher.md) · ★2)
- **strategy-researcher**: Investiga y diseña estrategias de trading para el bot y redacta su spec (hipótesis, universo, reglas, parámetros con rangos, comportamiento esperado por régimen, riesgos). Úsalo antes de escribir código de una estrategia nueva o de una versión nueva de una existente, y para contrastar una idea contra la literatura y contra lo que ya se probó en experiments/. Es read-only: devuelve el texto de la spec para que la sesión principal lo escriba en docs/strategy/. ([JoacoMarc/trading-bot](https://github.com/JoacoMarc/trading-bot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/strategy-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **strategy-researcher**: Проводит стратегический ресёрч по узкому конкретному вопросу — рынок, ниша, тренд, бенчмарки, JTBD-сегмент, growth-канал, ценовой коридор. Собирает цифры из открытых источников (приоритет — последние 24 месяца), перепроверяет каждую вторым источником, структурирует по авторитетному фреймворку. НЕ берётся за: досье по одному игроку (competitor-intel), go/no-go по уже собранным данным (decision-analyst), пересказ готового ресёрча в читаемый текст (interesting-narrator). ([kamilibragimov7772-lab/orchestrator](https://github.com/kamilibragimov7772-lab/orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/strategy-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **strategy-researcher**: Use this agent when you need to research and identify optimal strategies for current problems, technologies, or methodologies. - **strategy-researcher**: Designs setups combining flow features with context, writes hypotheses before testing, and validates walk-forward only. ([beatsbymaj/orderflowbot](https://github.com/beatsbymaj/orderflowbot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/strategy-researcher.md))

- **strategy-risk-analyst**: - **Fixed Fractional**: 거래당 자본의 N% 리스크 - 공식: Position Size = (Account × Risk%) / Per-Unit Risk - 예: $10,000 × 1% / $50 stop = 2 contracts - **ATR 기반 변동성 타겟팅**: 목표 변동성에 맞춘 동적 사이징 - 공식: Position Size = Target Vol / (ATR × Multiplier × Point Value) - **Kelly Criterion**: 기대값 기반 최적 비율 (half-Kelly 권장) - 공식: f* = (bp - q) / b (b=평균이익/평균손실, p=승률, q=1-p) - **고정 수량**: 단순 전략이나 모의매매용 ([kjs0807/JS_Repository](https://github.com/kjs0807/JS_Repository/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/strategy-risk-analyst.md))
- **strategy-risk-assessment**: Deloitte-level risk analysis and scenario planning for GatheredOS. Use when you need risk identification, probability/impact assessment, mitigation planning, or scenario modeling. ([kvadou/gatheredos](https://github.com/kvadou/gatheredos/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/strategy-risk-assessment.md))

- **strategy-time-state**: Seven stores, split by lifetime and rate: `appStore` (flow, squad, outcome), `missionStore` (HUD, pause), `worldStore` (clock, sectors, contracts), `researchStore`, `campaignStore` (roster, injuries, recruits, bay pins), `tutorialStore`, `settingsStore`.
